Luna reports user-directed remote fast-forward, preserving worker identities and
uncommitted state. Prior frozen board backed up byte-identically at
`/tmp/yulang-loop-a-board-20261016.md`, SHA-256
`9a49b5cac795ab8ea3661ce28c43f0d9e2a7fc18a1f0dc8724d2ef5415a23cea`.

with hash `9b2fa5aa73707383b4bac3db88254db77edf6ffade60b248378b604f6d8dfe4e`.
Both hashes verified before reconstruction. Backup is preserved, not disposable.
Current board seed is infrastructure, not evidence that existing workers vanished.

Dispatcher reread current root AGENTS, override, loop decision/addendum, both
skills, README and role registrations/pins. No Git, tests/builds/probes,
configuration changes, agent contact/launch/interrupt, cleanup or other file edit.
No worker assignments changed. Luna reports no active producer output/inbox
lease; producer artifacts frozen. No active build/probe process is reported;
complete OS process inventory remains unknown. Board is the only current write
lease reported, and is frozen on this return.

Current implementation role pins **gpt-6-luna/high minimum**; proof/dispatcher
normally Sol/high. This policy/config update does not hot-reload an existing
worker or prove its observed model. Preserve the legacy implementer idle; do not
send it code or relabel it Luna/high. No replacement/duplicate worker selected
while no implementation task is ready. For the next ready bounded code packet,
prefer explicit primary Luna/high execution under a validated exact lease,
preserving this worker. Alternatively Luna may establish a new pinned Luna/high
implementation executor after an explicit nonoverlapping handoff, retaining the
old identity parked; actual runtime routing must be reported. If neither supported
route meets the floor, implementation waits; never fall back to Sol. One-off
implementation_escalation requires actual Luna evidence, frozen/relinquished
lease and bounded Astra contract; none selected now. Configured total ceiling
12; available capacity/observed models unknown. No active Astra: E1-R2 completed and froze both outputs.

## Bounded native/runtime ownership audit and concrete I6 source defect

Native Simple-sub owner: candidate_source::LocalSourceForm::If emits BoolNegative
condition constraint at original CONDITION occurrence (slot50); existing
candidate_conditionals::every_condition_and_every_branch_reaches_its_consumer
requires candidate conflicts for integer conditions. ordinary_expression_source
covers successful conditional receivers and unsupported-source refusal, not actual
supported-type-conflict propagation on discarded expression roots. Approved G3
uses same native constructors/whole-source constraints on authentic expression
receivers; discard does not remove typechecking, including roots AFTER main.

Playground actual consumer: lib.rs::prepare correctly withholds output/runtime on
ANY candidate conflicts, but its source-range lookup iterates HIR items and accepts
ONLY Binding carriers. Thus every error whose original occurrence is in an

Algorithm/invariant: initialize an empty HashMap incrementally from requested
occurrence refs, each value None, deduplicating before scans. Do NOT collect
with exact E-size hint or reserve E entries when many errors share occurrences:
allocated map should grow with U unique requested keys, not E error records.
Empty request returns immediately. Set unresolved count to unique key count.
Walk HIR ITEMS in original order. For each Binding use actual DefinitionRoot;
for each Expression resolve actual expression_root; call existing local_source_for,
retaining exactly the old .ok().flatten/skipping behavior and definition fallback.
Walk that authentic local expressions slice in original order. Fill a requested
slot ONLY when still None, saving a BORROWED stored range; decrement unresolved
once and stop entire source walk when all keys are found. Missing/foreign requests
stay None after full walk. This preserves old FIRST matching carrier/expression,
not last HashMap insertion. No all-expression index or synthetic range.

prepare then keeps EXACT original error iteration/order, output.observe per
error, kind formatting, location_into invocation and unavailable-span fallback;
replace per-error nested scan/range clone with map lookup. Successful inference
allocates no index. Do not optimize line/column formatting here: location_into
still scans prefixes per rendered diagnostic, so no whole-diagnostic linearity
claim. Preserve all current caps and withholding behavior, no truncation/filter.

Exact local design/invariant: add private default Vec<ConstraintOccurrenceId>
checking_by_event to Graph, length equals events length for real retain_source
appends. Reserve new vector fallibly alongside existing events/map BEFORE
registration commits; same unavailable() collection error on reserve failure.
Create exact original ConstraintOccurrenceId ONCE, insert clone into unchanged
checking forward map, push same identity to vector at old events.len(), append
same Association. Arc-backed occurrence clone does not duplicate HIR/DefId
payload; no fabricated ID, new graph vertex, event order or checking slot.
Graph::bytes MUST include checked capacity bytes for new vector; existing
Frame::bytes already consumes Graph::bytes, so no unaccounted retained storage.
No new identity_bytes charge for Arc payload shared with old map; full vector
element storage counted. Keep original map and every forward consumer intact.

observed_checking(event) indexes vector and returns reference ONLY if unchanged
forward map still maps that exact checking identity to event. This validity
check is REQUIRED: repeat retain_source can overwrite a checking map entry;
old inverse scan then returns None for old event. Preserve that behavior rather
than assuming source uniqueness or returning stale identity. No fallback scan,
no new panic. For constructed graph, each stored event's original key is its
only possible forward-map inverse; append establishes, overwrite filters old
slot. Synthetic invalid map mutations are not new source/admission authority.
observed_value/vertices/fragment nodes/SCC/event endpoints unchanged.

Update only CandidateIntrusionObservation::events complexity comment: checking
lookup expected O(1) each (hash collisions worst O(E)); still Value descriptor
scans. Full iteration expected O(E*V+E), worst O(E*(V+E)); no whole renderer
linearity/constant-time worst-case or runtime measurement claim. Iteration still
allocates nothing; construction retains O(E) additional fixed-size elements
and amortized reserve work. No benchmark decision needed: budget0/0.
